cookiecutter/zipfile.py (module to test):
```
def unzip(
    zip_uri: str,
    is_url: bool,
    clone_to_dir: Path | str = ".",
    no_input: bool = False,
    password: str | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Download and unpack a zipfile at a given URI.

    This will download the zipfile to the cookiecutter repository,
    and unpack into a temporary directory.

    :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
    :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?
    :param clone_to_dir: The cookiecutter repository directory
        to put the archive into.
    :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
        cached resources.
    :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
    """
    # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
    clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()
    make_sure_path_exists(clone_to_dir)

    if is_url:
        # Build the name of the cached zipfile,
        # and prompt to delete if it already exists.
        identifier = zip_uri.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
        zip_path = os.path.join(clone_to_dir, identifier)

        if os.path.exists(zip_path):
            download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
        else:
            download = True

        if download:
            # (Re) download the zipfile
            r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
            with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
                    if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
                        f.write(chunk)
    else:
        # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
        zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)

    # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
    # into a temporary directory
    try:
        # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
        # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
        # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
        # locked until it is explicitly closed.
        with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
            if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
                msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
            # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
            first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
            if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
                msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # Construct the final target directory
            project_name = first_filename[:-1]
            unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)

            # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
            try:
                zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
            except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
                # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
                # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
                if password is not None:
                    try:
                        zip_file.extractall(
                            path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                        )
                    except RuntimeError as e:
                        msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
                elif no_input:
                    msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
                    raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
                else:
                    retry: int | None = 0
                    while retry is not None:
                        try:
                            password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
                            zip_file.extractall(
                                path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                            )
                            retry = None
                        except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
                            retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
                            if retry == 3:
                                msg = (
                                    'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                                )
                                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    except BadZipFile as e:
        msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    return unzip_path

```

# Prompt used at query #9

cookiecutter/utils.py:
```
def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.
    """
    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:
        Path(path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

```

cookiecutter/prompt.py:
```
def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API
    if no_input:
        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

    if ok_to_delete:
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)

```

def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)

        )

def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.

    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:

    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

cookiecutter/zipfile.py (module to test):
```
def unzip(
    zip_uri: str,
    is_url: bool,
    clone_to_dir: Path | str = ".",
    no_input: bool = False,
    password: str | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Download and unpack a zipfile at a given URI.

    This will download the zipfile to the cookiecutter repository,
    and unpack into a temporary directory.

    :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
    :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?
    :param clone_to_dir: The cookiecutter repository directory
        to put the archive into.
    :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
        cached resources.
    :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
    """
    # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
    clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()
    make_sure_path_exists(clone_to_dir)

    if is_url:
        # Build the name of the cached zipfile,
        # and prompt to delete if it already exists.
        identifier = zip_uri.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
        zip_path = os.path.join(clone_to_dir, identifier)

        if os.path.exists(zip_path):
            download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
        else:
            download = True

        if download:
            # (Re) download the zipfile
            r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
            with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
                    if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
                        f.write(chunk)
    else:
        # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
        zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)

    # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
    # into a temporary directory
    try:
        # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
        # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
        # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
        # locked until it is explicitly closed.
        with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
            if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
                msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
            # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
            first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
            if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
                msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # Construct the final target directory
            project_name = first_filename[:-1]
            unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)

            # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
            try:
                zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
            except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
                # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
                # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
                if password is not None:
                    try:
                        zip_file.extractall(
                            path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                        )
                    except RuntimeError as e:
                        msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
                elif no_input:
                    msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
                    raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
                else:
                    retry: int | None = 0
                    while retry is not None:
                        try:
                            password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
                            zip_file.extractall(
                                path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                            )
                            retry = None
                        except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
                            retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
                            if retry == 3:
                                msg = (
                                    'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                                )
                                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    except BadZipFile as e:
        msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    return unzip_path

```
Write unit test for function unzip

def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API
    if no_input:
        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

    if ok_to_delete:
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()

cookiecutter/utils.py:
```
def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.
    """
    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:
        Path(path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

```

 17 |     :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
 18 |         cached resources.
 19 |     :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
 20 |     """
 21 |     # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
 22 |     clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()

 30 |

 32 |             download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
 33 |         else:
 34 |             download = True
 35 | 
 36 |         if download:
 37 |             # (Re) download the zipfile
 38 |             r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
 39 |             with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
 40 |                 for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
 41 |                     if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
 42 |                         f.write(chunk)
 43 |     else:
 44 |         # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
 45 |         zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)
 46 | 
 47 |     # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
 48 |     # into a temporary directory
 49 |     try:
 50 |         # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
 51 |         # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
 52 |         # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
 53 |         # locked until it is explicitly closed.
 54 |         with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
 55 |             if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
 56 |                 msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
 57 |                 raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)
 58 | 
 59 |             # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
 60 |             # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
 61 |             first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
 62 |             if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
 63 |                 msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
 64 |                 raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)
 65 |

 67 |             project_name = first_filename[:-1]
 68 |             unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
 69 |             unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)
 70 | 
 71 |             # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
 72 |             try:
 73 |                 zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
 74 |             except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
 75 |                 # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
 76 |                 # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
 77 |                 if password is not None:
 78 |                     try:
 79 |                         zip_file.extractall(
 80 |                             path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
 81 |                         )
 82 |                     except RuntimeError as e:
 83 |                         msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
 84 |                         raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
 85 |                 elif no_input:
 86 |                     msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
 87 |                     raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
 88 |                 else:
 89 |                     retry: int | None = 0
 90 |                     while retry is not None:
 91 |                         try:
 92 |                             password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
 93 |                             zip_file.extractall(
 94 |                                 path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
 95 |                             )
 96 |                             retry = None
 97 |                         except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
 98 |                             retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
 99 |                             if retry == 3:
100 |                                 msg = (
101 |                                     'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
102 |                                 )
103 |                                 raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
104 | 
105 |     except BadZipFile as e:
106 |         msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
107 |         raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
108 | 
109 |     return unzip_path

def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API
    if no_input:
        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

    if ok_to_delete:
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()

def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.

 13 |     :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
 14 |     :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?
 15 |     :param clone_to_dir: The cookiecutter repository directory
 16 |         to put the archive into.
 17 |     :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
 18 |         cached resources.
 19 |     :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
 20 |     """
 21 |     # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
 22 |     clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()
 23 |     make_sure_path_exists(clone_to_dir)
 24 | 
 25 |     if is_url:
 26 |         # Build the name of the cached zipfile,
 27 |         # and prompt to delete if it already exists.
 28 |         identifier = zip_uri.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
 29 |         zip_path = os.path.join(clone_to_dir, identifier)
 30 | 
 31 |         if os.path.exists(zip_path):
 32 |             download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
 33 |         else:
 34 |             download = True
 35 | 
 36 |         if download:
 37 |             # (Re) download the zipfile
 38 |             r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
 39 |             with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
 40 |                 for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
 41 |                     if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
 42 |                         f.write(chunk)
 43 |     else:
 44 |         # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
 45 |         zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)
 46 | 
 47 |     # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
 48 |     # into a temporary directory
 49 |     try:
 50 |         # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
 51 |         # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
 52 |         # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
 53 |         # locked until it is explicitly closed.
 54 |         with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
 55 |             if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
 56 |                 msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
 57 |                 raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)
 58 | 
 59 |             # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
 60 |             # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
 61 |             first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
 62 |             if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
 63 |                 msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
 64 |                 raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)
 65 | 
 66 |             # Construct the final target directory
 67 |             project_name = first_filename[:-1]
 68 |             unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
 69 |             unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)
 70 | 
 71 |             # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
 72 |             try:
 73 |                 zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
 74 |             except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
 75 |                 # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
 76 |                 # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
 77 |                 if password is not None:
 78 |                     try:
 79 |                         zip_file.extractall(
 80 |                             path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
 81 |                         )
 82 |                     except RuntimeError as e:
 83 |                         msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
 84 |                         raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
 85 |                 elif no_input:
 86 |                     msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
 87 |                     raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
 88 |                 else:
 89 |                     retry: int | None = 0
 90 |                     while retry is not None:
 91 |                         try:
 92 |                             password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
 93 |                             zip_file.extractall(
 94 |                                 path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
 95 |                             )
 96 |                             retry = None
 97 |                         except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
 98 |                             retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
 99 |                             if retry == 3:
100 |                                 msg = (
101 |                                     'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
102 |                                 )
103 |                                 raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
104 | 
105 |     except BadZipFile as e:
106 |         msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
107 |         raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
108 | 
109 |     return unzip_path
```


# Prompt used at query #6
#--------------------------

cookiecutter/prompt.py:
```
def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API
    if no_input:
        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

    if ok_to_delete:
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()

```

cookiecutter/utils.py:
```
def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.
    """
    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:
        Path(path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

```

def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:

        else var_name

# Prompt used at query #16
#--------------------------

cookiecutter/prompt.py:
```
def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API
    if no_input:
        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

    if ok_to_delete:
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)

```

cookiecutter/utils.py:
```
def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.
    """
    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:
        Path(path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

```

cookiecutter/zipfile.py (module to test):
```
def unzip(
    zip_uri: str,
    is_url: bool,
    clone_to_dir: Path | str = ".",
    no_input: bool = False,
    password: str | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Download and unpack a zipfile at a given URI.

    This will download the zipfile to the cookiecutter repository,
    and unpack into a temporary directory.

    :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
    :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?
    :param clone_to_dir: The cookiecutter repository directory
        to put the archive into.
    :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
        cached resources.
    :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
    """
    # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
    clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()
    make_sure_path_exists(clone_to_dir)

    if is_url:
        # Build the name of the cached zipfile,
        # and prompt to delete if it already exists.
        identifier = zip_uri.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
        zip_path = os.path.join(clone_to_dir, identifier)

        if os.path.exists(zip_path):
            download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
        else:
            download = True

        if download:
            # (Re) download the zipfile
            r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
            with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
                    if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
                        f.write(chunk)
    else:
        # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
        zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)

    # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
    # into a temporary directory
    try:
        # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
        # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
        # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
        # locked until it is explicitly closed.
        with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
            if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
                msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
            # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
            first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
            if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
                msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # Construct the final target directory
            project_name = first_filename[:-1]
            unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)

            # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
            try:
                zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
            except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
                # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
                # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
                if password is not None:
                    try:
                        zip_file.extractall(
                            path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                        )
                    except RuntimeError as e:
                        msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
                elif no_input:
                    msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
                    raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
                else:
                    retry: int | None = 0
                    while retry is not None:
                        try:
                            password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
                            zip_file.extractall(
                                path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                            )
                            retry = None
                        except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
                            retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
                            if retry == 3:
                                msg = (
                                    'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                                )
                                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    except BadZipFile as e:
        msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    return unzip_path

```
Write unit test for function unzip

        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()

```

def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.
    """
    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:
        Path(path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

cookiecutter/zipfile.py (module to test):
```
def unzip(
    zip_uri: str,
    is_url: bool,
    clone_to_dir: Path | str = ".",
    no_input: bool = False,
    password: str | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Download and unpack a zipfile at a given URI.

    This will download the zipfile to the cookiecutter repository,
    and unpack into a temporary directory.

    :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
    :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?
    :param clone_to_dir: The cookiecutter repository directory
        to put the archive into.
    :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
        cached resources.
    :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
    """
    # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
    clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()
    make_sure_path_exists(clone_to_dir)

    if is_url:
        # Build the name of the cached zipfile,
        # and prompt to delete if it already exists.
        identifier = zip_uri.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
        zip_path = os.path.join(clone_to_dir, identifier)

        if os.path.exists(zip_path):
            download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
        else:
            download = True

        if download:
            # (Re) download the zipfile
            r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
            with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
                    if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
                        f.write(chunk)
    else:
        # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
        zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)

    # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
    # into a temporary directory
    try:
        # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
        # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
        # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
        # locked until it is explicitly closed.
        with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
            if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
                msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
            # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
            first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
            if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
                msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # Construct the final target directory
            project_name = first_filename[:-1]
            unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)

            # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
            try:
                zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
            except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
                # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
                # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
                if password is not None:
                    try:
                        zip_file.extractall(
                            path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                        )
                    except RuntimeError as e:
                        msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
                elif no_input:
                    msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
                    raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
                else:
                    retry: int | None = 0
                    while retry is not None:
                        try:
                            password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
                            zip_file.extractall(
                                path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                            )
                            retry = None
                        except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
                            retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
                            if retry == 3:
                                msg = (
                                    'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                                )
                                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    except BadZipFile as e:
        msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    return unzip_path

```

cookiecutter/prompt.py:
```
def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API
    if no_input:
        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

    if ok_to_delete:
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()

```

    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:

    is_url: bool,
    clone_to_dir: Path | str = ".",
    no_input: bool = False,
    password: str | None = None,

    and unpack into a temporary directory.

    :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
    :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?

    :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of

                        )

                    raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err

                    while retry is not None:
                        try:
                            password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
                            zip_file.extractall(
                                path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                            )
                            retry = None
                        except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
                            retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
                            if retry == 3:
                                msg = (
                                    'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                                )
                                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    except BadZipFile as e:
        msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    return unzip_path

```

cookiecutter/prompt.py:
```
def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API
    if no_input:
        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

    if ok_to_delete:
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)

cookiecutter/utils.py:
```
def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.
    """
    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:
        Path(path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

```

def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:

cookiecutter/zipfile.py (module to test):
```
def unzip(
    zip_uri: str,
    is_url: bool,
    clone_to_dir: Path | str = ".",
    no_input: bool = False,
    password: str | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Download and unpack a zipfile at a given URI.

    This will download the zipfile to the cookiecutter repository,
    and unpack into a temporary directory.

    :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
    :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?
    :param clone_to_dir: The cookiecutter repository directory
        to put the archive into.
    :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
        cached resources.
    :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
    """
    # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
    clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()
    make_sure_path_exists(clone_to_dir)

    if is_url:
        # Build the name of the cached zipfile,
        # and prompt to delete if it already exists.
        identifier = zip_uri.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
        zip_path = os.path.join(clone_to_dir, identifier)

        if os.path.exists(zip_path):
            download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
        else:
            download = True

        if download:
            # (Re) download the zipfile
            r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
            with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
                    if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
                        f.write(chunk)
    else:
        # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
        zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)

    # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
    # into a temporary directory
    try:
        # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
        # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
        # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
        # locked until it is explicitly closed.
        with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
            if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
                msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
            # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
            first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
            if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
                msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # Construct the final target directory
            project_name = first_filename[:-1]
            unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)

            # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
            try:
                zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
            except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
                # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
                # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
                if password is not None:
                    try:
                        zip_file.extractall(
                            path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                        )
                    except RuntimeError as e:
                        msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
                elif no_input:
                    msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
                    raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
                else:
                    retry: int | None = 0
                    while retry is not None:
                        try:
                            password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
                            zip_file.extractall(
                                path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                            )
                            retry = None
                        except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
                            retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
                            if retry == 3:
                                msg = (
                                    'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                                )
                                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    except BadZipFile as e:
        msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    return unzip_path

```
Write unit test for function unzip

# Prompt used at query #13

cookiecutter/utils.py:
```
def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.
    """
    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:
        Path(path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

```

cookiecutter/prompt.py:
```
def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API
    if no_input:
        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

    if ok_to_delete:
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()

```

def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API

        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.
    """
    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:
        Path(path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

cookiecutter/zipfile.py (module to test):
```
def unzip(
    zip_uri: str,
    is_url: bool,
    clone_to_dir: Path | str = ".",
    no_input: bool = False,
    password: str | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Download and unpack a zipfile at a given URI.

    This will download the zipfile to the cookiecutter repository,
    and unpack into a temporary directory.

    :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
    :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?
    :param clone_to_dir: The cookiecutter repository directory
        to put the archive into.
    :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
        cached resources.
    :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
    """
    # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
    clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()
    make_sure_path_exists(clone_to_dir)

    if is_url:
        # Build the name of the cached zipfile,
        # and prompt to delete if it already exists.
        identifier = zip_uri.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
        zip_path = os.path.join(clone_to_dir, identifier)

        if os.path.exists(zip_path):
            download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
        else:
            download = True

        if download:
            # (Re) download the zipfile
            r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
            with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
                    if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
                        f.write(chunk)
    else:
        # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
        zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)

    # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
    # into a temporary directory
    try:
        # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
        # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
        # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
        # locked until it is explicitly closed.
        with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
            if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
                msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
            # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
            first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
            if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
                msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # Construct the final target directory
            project_name = first_filename[:-1]
            unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)

            # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
            try:
                zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
            except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
                # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
                # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
                if password is not None:
                    try:
                        zip_file.extractall(
                            path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                        )
                    except RuntimeError as e:
                        msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
                elif no_input:
                    msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
                    raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
                else:
                    retry: int | None = 0
                    while retry is not None:
                        try:
                            password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
                            zip_file.extractall(
                                path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                            )
                            retry = None
                        except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
                            retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
                            if retry == 3:
                                msg = (
                                    'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                                )
                                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    except BadZipFile as e:
        msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    return unzip_path

```
Write unit test for function unzip

# Prompt used at query #15

cookiecutter/prompt.py:
```
def read_user_yes_no(var_name, default_value, prompts=None, prefix: str = ""):
    """Prompt the user to reply with 'yes' or 'no' (or equivalent values).

    - These input values will be converted to ``True``:
      "1", "true", "t", "yes", "y", "on"
    - These input values will be converted to ``False``:
      "0", "false", "f", "no", "n", "off"

    Actual parsing done by :func:`prompt`; Check this function codebase change in
    case of unexpected behaviour.

    :param str question: Question to the user
    :param default_value: Value that will be returned if no input happens
    """
    question = (
        prompts[var_name]
        if prompts and var_name in prompts and prompts[var_name]
        else var_name
    )
    return YesNoPrompt.ask(f"{prefix}{question}", default=default_value)


def read_repo_password(question: str) -> str:
    """Prompt the user to enter a password.

    :param question: Question to the user
    """
    return Prompt.ask(question, password=True)


def prompt_and_delete(path: Path | str, no_input: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Ask user if it's okay to delete the previously-downloaded file/directory.

    If yes, delete it. If no, checks to see if the old version should be
    reused. If yes, it's reused; otherwise, Cookiecutter exits.

    :param path: Previously downloaded zipfile.
    :param no_input: Suppress prompt to delete repo and just delete it.
    :return: True if the content was deleted
    """
    # Suppress prompt if called via API
    if no_input:
        ok_to_delete = True
    else:
        question = (
            f"You've downloaded {path} before. Is it okay to delete and re-download it?"
        )

        ok_to_delete = read_user_yes_no(question, 'yes')

    if ok_to_delete:
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            rmtree(path)
        else:
            os.remove(path)
        return True
    ok_to_reuse = read_user_yes_no("Do you want to re-use the existing version?", 'yes')

    if ok_to_reuse:
        return False

    sys.exit()

```

cookiecutter/utils.py:
```
def rmtree(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Remove a directory and all its contents. Like rm -rf on Unix.

    :param path: A directory path.
    """
    shutil.rmtree(path, onerror=force_delete)


def make_sure_path_exists(path: Path | str) -> None:
    """Ensure that a directory exists.

    :param path: A directory tree path for creation.
    """
    logger.debug('Making sure path exists (creates tree if not exist): %s', path)
    try:
        Path(path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as error:
        msg = f'Unable to create directory at {path}'
        raise OSError(msg) from error

```

cookiecutter/zipfile.py (module to test):
```
def unzip(
    zip_uri: str,
    is_url: bool,
    clone_to_dir: Path | str = ".",
    no_input: bool = False,
    password: str | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Download and unpack a zipfile at a given URI.

    This will download the zipfile to the cookiecutter repository,
    and unpack into a temporary directory.

    :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
    :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?
    :param clone_to_dir: The cookiecutter repository directory
        to put the archive into.
    :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
        cached resources.
    :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
    """
    # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
    clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()
    make_sure_path_exists(clone_to_dir)

    if is_url:
        # Build the name of the cached zipfile,
        # and prompt to delete if it already exists.
        identifier = zip_uri.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
        zip_path = os.path.join(clone_to_dir, identifier)

        if os.path.exists(zip_path):
            download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
        else:
            download = True

        if download:
            # (Re) download the zipfile
            r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
            with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
                    if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
                        f.write(chunk)
    else:
        # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
        zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)

    # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
    # into a temporary directory
    try:
        # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
        # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
        # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
        # locked until it is explicitly closed.
        with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
            if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
                msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
            # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
            first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
            if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
                msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # Construct the final target directory
            project_name = first_filename[:-1]
            unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)

            # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
            try:
                zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
            except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
                # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
                # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
                if password is not None:
                    try:
                        zip_file.extractall(
                            path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                        )
                    except RuntimeError as e:
                        msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
                elif no_input:
                    msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
                    raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
                else:
                    retry: int | None = 0
                    while retry is not None:
                        try:
                            password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
                            zip_file.extractall(
                                path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                            )
                            retry = None
                        except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
                            retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
                            if retry == 3:
                                msg = (
                                    'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                                )
                                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    except BadZipFile as e:
        msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    return unzip_path

```
Write unit test for function unzip

def unzip(
    zip_uri: str,
    is_url: bool,
    clone_to_dir: Path | str = ".",
    no_input: bool = False,
    password: str | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Download and unpack a zipfile at a given URI.

    This will download the zipfile to the cookiecutter repository,
    and unpack into a temporary directory.

    :param zip_uri: The URI for the zipfile.
    :param is_url: Is the zip URI a URL or a file?
    :param clone_to_dir: The cookiecutter repository directory
        to put the archive into.
    :param no_input: Do not prompt for user input and eventually force a refresh of
        cached resources.
    :param password: The password to use when unpacking the repository.
    """
    # Ensure that clone_to_dir exists
    clone_to_dir = Path(clone_to_dir).expanduser()
    make_sure_path_exists(clone_to_dir)

    if is_url:
        # Build the name of the cached zipfile,
        # and prompt to delete if it already exists.
        identifier = zip_uri.rsplit('/', 1)[1]
        zip_path = os.path.join(clone_to_dir, identifier)

        if os.path.exists(zip_path):
            download = prompt_and_delete(zip_path, no_input=no_input)
        else:
            download = True

        if download:
            # (Re) download the zipfile
            r = requests.get(zip_uri, stream=True, timeout=100)
            with open(zip_path, 'wb') as f:
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024):
                    if chunk:  # filter out keep-alive new chunks
                        f.write(chunk)
    else:
        # Just use the local zipfile as-is.
        zip_path = os.path.abspath(zip_uri)

    # Now unpack the repository. The zipfile will be unpacked
    # into a temporary directory
    try:
        # Use context manager so the file descriptor is always released, even if
        # an exception occurs while processing the archive. This prevents file
        # handle leaks on operating systems that keep the underlying ZIP file
        # locked until it is explicitly closed.
        with ZipFile(zip_path) as zip_file:
            if len(zip_file.namelist()) == 0:
                msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is empty'
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # The first record in the zipfile should be the directory entry for
            # the archive. If it isn't a directory, there's a problem.
            first_filename = zip_file.namelist()[0]
            if not first_filename.endswith('/'):
                msg = f"Zip repository {zip_uri} does not include a top-level directory"
                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg)

            # Construct the final target directory
            project_name = first_filename[:-1]
            unzip_base = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            unzip_path = os.path.join(unzip_base, project_name)

            # Extract the zip file into the temporary directory
            try:
                zip_file.extractall(path=unzip_base)
            except RuntimeError as runtime_err:
                # File is password protected; try to get a password from the
                # environment; if that doesn't work, ask the user.
                if password is not None:
                    try:
                        zip_file.extractall(
                            path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                        )
                    except RuntimeError as e:
                        msg = 'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e
                elif no_input:
                    msg = 'Unable to unlock password protected repository'
                    raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from runtime_err
                else:
                    retry: int | None = 0
                    while retry is not None:
                        try:
                            password = read_repo_password('Repo password')
                            zip_file.extractall(
                                path=unzip_base, pwd=password.encode('utf-8')
                            )
                            retry = None
                        except RuntimeError as e:  # noqa: PERF203
                            retry += 1  # type: ignore[operator]
                            if retry == 3:
                                msg = (
                                    'Invalid password provided for protected repository'
                                )
                                raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    except BadZipFile as e:
        msg = f'Zip repository {zip_uri} is not a valid zip archive:'
        raise InvalidZipRepository(msg) from e

    return unzip_path
